The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated in extensive constrained DLO manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [121] [Coordinated Humanoid Manipulation with Choice Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25072)
*Haozhi Qi,Yen-Jen Wang,Toru Lin,Brent Yi,Yi Ma,Koushil Sreenath,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合模块化遥操作界面与可扩展学习框架的系统，用于解决人形机器人全身协调控制问题，在洗碗机装载和白板擦拭任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类中心环境中具有巨大潜力，但实现头、手、腿之间的鲁棒全身协调仍然是一个重大挑战。需要开发能够有效收集高质量演示数据并学习复杂多模态行为的系统。

Method: 1) 模块化遥操作设计：将人形控制分解为直观子模块，包括手眼协调、抓取基元、手臂末端执行器跟踪和运动控制；2) Choice Policy：一种模仿学习方法，生成多个候选动作并学习对它们进行评分，实现快速推理和多模态行为建模。

Result: 在真实世界任务中验证：洗碗机装载和全身运动操作（白板擦拭）。Choice Policy显著优于扩散策略和标准行为克隆。实验表明手眼协调对于长时域任务的成功至关重要。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在非结构化环境中实现可扩展数据收集和人形机器人协调操作的实用路径，模块化遥操作与Choice Policy的结合为解决复杂人形控制问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise for operating in human-centric environments, yet achieving robust whole-body coordination across the head, hands, and legs remains a major challenge. We present a system that combines a modular teleoperation interface with a scalable learning framework to address this problem. Our teleoperation design decomposes humanoid control into intuitive submodules, which include hand-eye coordination, grasp primitives, arm end-effector tracking, and locomotion. This modularity allows us to collect high-quality demonstrations efficiently. Building on this, we introduce Choice Policy, an imitation learning approach that generates multiple candidate actions and learns to score them. This architecture enables both fast inference and effective modeling of multimodal behaviors. We validate our approach on two real-world tasks: dishwasher loading and whole-body loco-manipulation for whiteboard wiping. Experiments show that Choice Policy significantly outperforms diffusion policies and standard behavior cloning. Furthermore, our results indicate that hand-eye coordination is critical for success in long-horizon tasks. Our work demonstrates a practical path toward scalable data collection and learning for coordinated humanoid manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Yu-Shiba-Rusinov bound states of exciton condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23724)
*SeongJin Kwon,Kyung-Hwan Jin,Jong Eun Han,Siwon Lee,ChoongJae Won,Sang-Wook Cheong,Han Woong Yeom*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Ta2Pd3Te5范德华晶体中，首次直接观测到激子凝聚相中由杂质诱导的类Yu-Shiba-Rusinov束缚态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索激子凝聚相中是否存在类似超导体中Yu-Shiba-Rusinov束缚态的物理现象，这类现象虽在理论上预测了几十年但一直未被实验证实

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜和扫描隧道谱技术，在Ta2Pd3Te5晶体中直接可视化激子凝聚相中的带隙内电子态，并通过局部应变和载流子注入系统调控激子带隙

Result: 观测到带隙内态的能量与激子带隙强烈相关，理论分析表明这些态是由Ta空位相关的电荷偶极子通过电荷-激子版本的YSR机制诱导产生的

Conclusion: 该研究首次证实了激子凝聚相中的YSR物理，并建立了一种新的微观工具来探测和控制室温下持续存在的激子凝聚相的量子特性

Abstract: Quantum condensed states in solids often reveal their fundamental nature via interactions with impurities, as epitomized by Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) bound states at magnetic impurities in superconductors. Although analogous YSR bound states were predicted within quantum condensates of excitons several decades ago, their existence has been elusive. Here, we directly visualize in-gap electronic states bound to impurities inside an exciton condensate phase of a van der Waals crystal Ta2Pd3Te5, utilizing scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy. We find that the energies of in-gap states are strongly correlated with the excitonic band gap, which is systematically tuned by local strain and carrier injection. Our theoretical analyses reveal that these in-gap states are induced by charge dipoles associated with Ta vacancies through a charge-exciton version of the YSR mechanism. Our findings establish both the YSR physics in exciton condensates and a novel microscopic tool to probe and control quantum properties in exciton condensates persisting up to room temperature.

</details>


### [123] [Hedgehog lattices induced by chiral spin interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23798)
*Ryan Mays,Predrag Nikolić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了简单立方晶格上的经典海森堡自旋模型，包含时间反演不变性和多重手性自旋相互作用。通过蒙特卡洛模拟发现了稳健的4Q二分晶格结构（刺猬和反刺猬），并观察到一级相变熔化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于理解在MnGe、MnSi₁₋ₓGeₓ和SrFeO₃等材料中观察到的刺猬晶格现象。这些材料中的局部磁矩与巡游外尔电子耦合，或具有强自旋轨道耦合的局域电子，在远离半填充时会产生外尔谱。需要建立理论模型来解释这些拓扑磁结构。

Method: 采用经典海森堡自旋模型，该模型具有时间反演不变性并包含多重手性自旋相互作用。使用蒙特卡洛方法进行数值模拟，研究简单立方晶格上的自旋动力学。模型适用于两种物理情景：局部磁矩与巡游外尔电子耦合，或具有强自旋轨道耦合的局域电子。

Result: 发现了稳健的4Q二分晶格结构，由刺猬和反刺猬组成。该结构在相图的某些区域通过一级相变在临界温度熔化。刺猬密度是Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的非线性函数，但却是多重自旋手性相互作用的线性函数。多重自旋手性相互作用起着"磁通量"或刺猬化学势的基本作用。

Conclusion: 研究结果与MnGe、MnSi₁₋ₓGeₓ和SrFeO₃中观察到的刺猬晶格现象相关，间接支持了不可压缩量子无序刺猬液体可能存在的观点。多重自旋手性相互作用作为拓扑磁结构的控制参数具有关键作用。

Abstract: We analyze a classical Heisenberg spin model on the simple cubic lattice which is invariant under time reversal and contains multiple chiral spin interactions. The modelled dynamics is appropriate either for local moments coupled to itinerant Weyl electrons, or localized electrons with a strong spin-orbit coupling that would produce a Weyl spectrum away from half filling. Using a Monte Carlo method, we find a robust $4Q$ bipartite lattice of hedgehogs and antihedgehogs which melts through a first order phase transition at a critical temperature in certain segments of the phase diagram. The density of hedgehogs is a non-linear function of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, and a linear function of the multiple-spin chiral interaction which plays the fundamental role of a ``magnetic flux'' or a hedgehog chemical potential. These findings are related to the observations of hedgehog lattices in MnGe, MnSi$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$ and SrFeO$_3$, and indirectly support the possible existence of incompressible quantum-disordered hedgehog liquids.

</details>


### [124] [Origin of insulating state in bulk $1T$-TaS$_2$ revealed by out-of-plane dimerization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23883)
*Achyut Tiwari,Maxim Wenzel,Renjith Mathew Roy,Christian Prange,Bruno Gompf,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过红外光谱揭示了1T-TaS₂中电荷密度波相的层内和层间电荷动力学差异，发现层间响应由准一维Peierls型二聚化主导，该二聚化是金属-绝缘体转变的主要驱动机理，而非电子关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究1T-TaS₂中电荷密度波相的电荷动力学特性，特别是层内和层间方向的差异，以理解金属-绝缘体转变的微观机制，澄清电子关联效应与结构畸变在相变中的相对重要性。

Method: 采用温度和偏振依赖的红外光谱技术，结合密度泛函理论计算，分析1T-TaS₂在电荷密度波相中的光学响应，特别关注平行和垂直于层方向的电荷动力学差异。

Result: 实验发现层间响应由准一维Peierls型二聚化主导，这种结构畸变是金属-绝缘体转变的主要驱动机理；密度泛函理论计算支持这一结论，表明电子关联效应在相变中作用不显著。

Conclusion: 1T-TaS₂中的金属-绝缘体转变主要由层间Peierls型二聚化驱动，而非电子关联效应，这澄清了该材料体系相变机制的长久争议。

Abstract: The commensurate charge-density-wave phase in the protoypical transition metal dichalcogenide $1T$-TaS$_2$ is investigated by temperature and polarization-dependent infrared spectroscopy revealing the fundamentally different charge dynamics parallel and perpendicular to the layers. Supported by density-functional-theory calculations, we demonstrate that the out-of-plane response is governed by a quasi-one-dimensional, Peierls-like dimerization of the two-dimensional star-of-David layers. In particular, our results identifies this dimerization as the primary driving mechanism of the metal-to-insulator transition, ruling out a significant role of electronic correlations.

</details>


### [125] [Fragile Topological Phases and Topological Order of 2D Crystalline Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24709)
*Hisham Sati,Urs Schreiber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文应用等变同伦理论方法，首次对二维晶体陈绝缘体材料的脆弱/不稳定拓扑相及其分数化版本的可能拓扑序进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管晶体陈绝缘体受到广泛关注，但之前未考虑使用等变同伦理论方法，特别是等变2-余同伦理论来分类其拓扑相。论文旨在填补这一方法学空白，并为寻找拓扑量子计算硬件提供理论基础。

Method: 应用等变同伦理论方法，将拓扑相分类问题转化为计算布里渊环面上（相对于壁纸点群作用）的等变2-余同伦。通过计算绝热单值群来研究拓扑序，特别关注非阿贝尔群情况。

Result: 发现晶体陈绝缘体的拓扑相由布里渊环面上的等变2-余同伦描述。在非阿贝尔群示例中，计算表明任何潜在的分数陈绝缘体任意子必须在动量空间中局域化。

Conclusion: 等变同伦理论为晶体拓扑相分类提供了新的数学框架，揭示了分数陈绝缘体任意子的动量空间局域化特性，这对拓扑量子计算硬件的搜索具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We apply methods of equivariant homotopy theory, which may not previously have found due attention in condensed matter physics, to classify first the fragile/unstable topological phases of 2D crystalline Chern insulator materials, and second the possible topological order of their fractional cousins. We highlight that the phases are given by the equivariant 2-Cohomotopy of the Brillouin torus of crystal momenta (with respect to wallpaper point group actions) -- which, despite the attention devoted to crystalline Chern insulators, seems not to have been considered before. Arguing then that any topological order must be reflected in the adiabatic monodromy of gapped quantum ground states over the covariantized space of these band topologies, we compute the latter in examples where this group is non-abelian, showing that any potential FQAH anyons must be localized in momentum space. We close with an outlook on the relevance for the search for topological quantum computing hardware. Mathematical details are spelled out in a supplement.

</details>


### [126] [Correlated 5f electronic states and phase stability in americium under high pressure: Insights from DFT+DMFT calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23963)
*Haiyan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 使用DFT+DMFT方法研究镅在100GPa压力下的四个高压相电子结构，发现5f电子在高压下从局域态向部分离域态转变，但仍比U和Pu更局域，高压相稳定性由Peierls-like畸变机制控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究镅在不同高压相中的电子结构演化，特别是5f电子的局域-离域转变，理解高压相变机制和量子现象，为锕系材料高压行为提供微观视角。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合嵌入式动力学平均场理论，研究镅的四个高压相(Am-I, Am-II, Am-III, Am-IV)在100GPa压力范围内的电子结构。

Result: 成功再现了Am-I相中-2.8eV处的局域5f峰；Am-I和Am-II相中5f电子保持强局域性，而Am-III和Am-IV相中5f电子出现增强的杂化特征：谱权重向费米能级移动、杂化强度增强、多峰结构出现；发现压力增强的价态涨落(5f5,5f6,5f7混合)；X射线吸收分支比显示角动量耦合接近jj极限；低对称高压相稳定性由Peierls-like畸变机制控制。

Conclusion: 镅在高压下5f电子从局域态向部分离域态转变，但仍比铀和钚更局域；高压相稳定性由对称性降低的晶格畸变和电子重构共同决定；该研究为锕系材料高压相变和量子现象提供了新的微观视角。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic structure of americium (Am) across its four experimentally confirmed high-pressure phases Am-I (P63/mmc), Am-II (Fm-3m), Am-III (Fddd), and Am-IV (Pnma) up to 100 GPa, using density functional theory combined with embedded dynamical mean-field theory. Our results successfully reproduce the prominent localized 5f peak observed in ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy around -2.8 eV below the Fermi level in the Am-I phase. While 5f electrons in Am-I and Am-II remain strongly localized, those in Am-III and Am-IV manifest discernible signatures of increased hybridization: a noticeable shift of spectral weight toward the Fermi level, enhanced hybridization strength, and the emergence of distinct multi-peak structures. These changes indicate that 5f electrons begin to participate in bonding and undergo partial delocalization under pressure. Nevertheless, the spectral weight of 5f electrons near the Fermi level in Am-IV remains relatively low, indicating that, compared to U and Pu, Am retains stronger localized 5f electrons even under high pressure. Analysis of the electronic configurations reveals pressure-enhanced valence state fluctuation, characterized by the mixing of 5f5, 5f6, and 5f7 electronic configurations. The X-ray absorption branching ratio further shows that the angular-momentum coupling scheme approaches the jj limit. Additionally, we demonstrate that the stability of the low-symmetry high-pressure phases (Am-III and Am-IV) is governed by a Peierls-like distortion mechanism, which reduces the total energy through symmetry-lowering lattice distortions accompanied by electronic reconstruction. This study offers a new microscopic perspective on high-pressure phase transitions and emergent quantum phenomena in actinide materials.

</details>


### [127] [Skyrmion and Meron Crystals in Intermetallic Gd$_3$Ru$_4$Al$_{12}$: Microscopic Model Insights into Chiral Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24071)
*Jiajun Mo,Leandro M. Chinellato,Fletcher Williams,Akiko Kikkawa,Joseph A. M. Paddison,Matthias D. Frontzek,Gabriele Sala,Chris Pasco,Kipton Barros,Taro Nakajima,Taka-hisa Arima,Yasujiro Taguchi,Yoshinori Tokura,Matthew B. Stone,Andrew D. Christianson,Cristian D. Batista,Shang Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过结合中子散射实验与理论建模，构建了Gd₃Ru₄Al₁₂的微观模型，揭示了偶极相互作用与易面单离子各向异性竞争是稳定手性磁相的关键，发现了六角斯格明子晶体和两种不同的麦纫晶体，并证实了螺旋自旋液体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受挫金属间化合物中拓扑自旋织构的起源和性质，这些织构在自旋电子学应用中具有巨大潜力，但由于传导电子介导的竞争性长程相互作用而难以理解。

Method: 结合中子散射实验与理论建模，采用前所未有的多目标拟合方法，进一步整合铁磁共振数据和磁化曲线，构建了Gd₃Ru₄Al₁₂的微观模型。

Result: 1. 识别出偶极相互作用与易面单离子各向异性竞争是稳定手性磁相的关键因素；2. 发现了六角斯格明子晶体和两种不同的麦纫晶体；3. 证实低场下的麦纫晶体与晶格共格，其独特的"三麦纫-一反麦纫"自旋织构被极化X射线衍射数据验证；4. 高温下的短程自旋关联可用二维螺旋自旋液体描述；5. 交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用引起的扰动解释了反常霍尔响应的温度和场依赖性。

Conclusion: 中子散射与互补实验技术结合具有独特能力，能够解析复杂的磁相变，并为受挫系统中丰富的拓扑自旋织构提供新见解，强调了多种相互作用竞争在稳定拓扑磁相中的重要性。

Abstract: Topological spin textures in frustrated intermetallics hold great promise for spintronics applications. However, understanding their origin and properties remains a significant challenge due to competing and often long-range interactions mediated by conduction electrons. Here, by combining neutron scattering experiments with theoretical modeling via unprecedented multi-target fits that further incorporate the ferromagnentic resonance data and magnetization curve, we construct a realistic microscopic model for the prototypical intermetallic skyrmion host \text{Gd}$_3$\text{Ru}$_4$\text{Al}$_{12}$. Beyond magnetic frustration, we identify the competition between dipolar interactions and easy-plane single-ion anisotropy as a key ingredient for stabilizing the rich chiral magnetic phases observed in this compound -- including a hexagonal skyrmion crystal and two distinct meron crystals. Remarkably, the meron crystal in lower field is revealed to be commensurate with the underlying lattice, and its unique three-meron-one-antimeron spin texture is verified by the polarized x-ray diffraction data. At elevated temperatures, the short-range spin correlations in \text{Gd}$_3$\text{Ru}$_4$\text{Al}$_{12}$ are well described by a codimension-two spiral spin-liquid. Perturbations from staggered Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions give rise to chiral fluctuations that account for the temperature and field dependence of the anomalous Hall response. Our results highlight the unique power of neutron scattering, especially when combined with complementary experimental techniques, to unravel complex magnetic phase transitions and provide new insights into the rich variety of topological spin textures in frustrated systems.

</details>


### [128] [Exactly Solvable Models Hosting Altermagnetic Quantum Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24076)
*João Augusto Sobral,Pietro M. Bonetti,Subrata Mandal,Mathias S. Scheurer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文构建了自旋-3/2和自旋-7/2模型，通过Majorana表示精确求解，发现了具有完整自旋转动和晶格平移对称性但破坏时间反演对称性的自旋液体相，称为反铁磁自旋液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有非平凡拓扑和对称性特征的新型量子相，探索反铁磁自旋液体这一新颖物质态，理解其丰富的物理特性。

Method: 在方-八边形和棋盘格晶格上构建自旋-3/2和自旋-7/2模型，使用Majorana表示进行精确求解，分析对称性破缺模式和拓扑激发。

Result: 自旋-3/2模型产生唯一的g波反铁磁自旋液体基态；自旋-7/2模型呈现更丰富的相图，包含不同类型的拓扑手性自旋液体与d波反铁磁自旋液体竞争；识别了拓扑和非拓扑激发。

Conclusion: 反铁磁自旋液体展示了非平凡拓扑和对称性相互作用的丰富物理，为探索新型量子物质态提供了重要范例，揭示了这种新颖相在拓扑和对称性方面的独特特征。

Abstract: We construct spin-$3/2$ and spin-$7/2$ models on the square-octagon and checkerboard lattices that are exactly solvable with Majorana representations. They give rise to spin-liquid phases with full spin-rotation and lattice-translational symmetries but broken time-reversal symmetry. Although non-zero on elementary plaquettes, the net orbital magnetic moment is guaranteed to vanish as a result of point symmetries; due to the analogy to long-range ordered altermagnets, these types of phases were dubbed altermagnetic spin liquids in [Phys. Rev. Research 7, 023152 (2025)]. For the spin-$3/2$ model, we find that a $g$-wave altermagnetic spin liquid emerges as the unique ground state. In contrast, the spin-7/2 model exhibits a significantly richer phase diagram, involving different types of chiral spin liquids competing with a $d$-wave altermagnetic spin liquid. Finally, we identify and characterize the topological and non-topological excitations, illustrating the rich physics of altermagnetic spin liquids resulting from the interplay of non-trivial topological and symmetry aspects of this novel phase of matter.

</details>


### [129] [Detection of a Rényi Index Dependent Transition in Entanglement Entropy Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24533)
*Hatem Barghathi,Adrian Del Maestro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了冯·诺依曼熵与二阶Rényi熵在纠缠标度行为上可能出现显著差异，并提出了基于电荷分辨二阶Rényi熵的冯·诺依曼熵下界诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冯·诺依曼纠缠熵的测量在实验和模拟中成本高昂，通常需要完全态层析。二阶Rényi熵虽然易于通过双拷贝协议测量，但通常假设其标度行为与冯·诺依曼熵一致。然而，先前研究表明在某些自旋模型基态中，不同Rényi指数的熵标度行为可能从幂律变化到对数标度。本文旨在探索这种标度差异的机制，并提供实验可访问的诊断方法。

Method: 首先构造了一个具有两个局域自由度的数守恒多体态，展示其二阶Rényi熵保持对数标度，而冯·诺依曼熵参数化更大的现象。然后引入了一个基于对称性的冯·诺依曼熵下界，该下界由电荷分辨二阶Rényi熵和子系统电荷分布构建。通过比较该下界与总二阶Rényi熵，提供了一种实用的异常纠缠标度诊断方法。

Result: 发现Rényi指数依赖的纠缠标度变化：在某些多体态中，二阶Rényi熵保持对数标度，而冯·诺依曼熵显著更大。提出的对称感知下界能够从实验可访问的数据中诊断异常纠缠标度行为，为实验研究提供了实用工具。

Conclusion: 冯·诺依曼熵与二阶Rényi熵的标度行为可能存在显著差异，不能简单假设后者追踪前者的渐近标度。基于电荷分辨二阶Rényi熵构建的冯·诺依曼熵下界提供了一种实验可行的诊断方法，可用于检测异常纠缠标度现象，对理解多体系统的纠缠结构和临界行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: The scaling of entanglement with subsystem size encodes key information about phases and criticality, but the von Neumann entropy is costly to access in experiments and simulations, often requiring full state tomography. The second Rényi entropy is readily measured using two-copy protocols and is often used as a proxy for the von Neumann entanglement entropy, where it is assumed to track its asymptotic scaling. However, Sugino and Korepiny (Int. J. Mod. Phys. B 32, 1850306 (2018)) revealed that in the ground state of some spin models, the scaling of the von Neumann and second Rényi entropies varies from power law to logarithmic scaling as a function of the Rényi index. By constructing a number-conserving many-body state with only two local degrees of freedom, we obtain a Rényi-index-dependent change in the leading entanglement scaling: the second Rényi entropy remains logarithmic while the von Neumann entropy is parametrically larger. We then introduce a symmetry-aware lower bound on the von Neumann entropy built from charge-resolved second Rényi entropies and the subsystem charge distribution. Comparing this bound to the total second Rényi entropy provides a practical diagnostic for anomalous entanglement scaling from experimentally accessible data.

</details>


### [130] [Disentangle Intertwined Interactions in Correlated Charge Density Wave with Magnetic Impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24668)
*J. W. Park,H. Kim,H. W. Yeom*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Fe原子在1T-TaS2的团簇Mott电荷密度波系统中表现出显著的位点依赖性电子态，揭示了与团簇Mott态的不同局域耦合机制，包括轨道杂化、CDW团簇畸变和电荷转移三种相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联电子系统中磁性杂质的微观作用机制，特别是位点特异性相互作用的物理本质。传统单点Kondo杂质模型在描述团簇Mott绝缘体中的杂质行为存在局限性，需要更精细的微观理解。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）实验和密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究单个Fe原子在1T-TaS2团簇Mott电荷密度波系统上的行为。

Result: 发现Fe原子在CDW团簇不同位置表现出显著不同的电子态，揭示了三种相互作用机制：与局域化关联电子的杂化、CDW团簇畸变、电荷转移。特别地，Fe 3d轨道与半填充Ta 5dz2轨道的杂化会抑制CDW团簇中心的Mott绝缘态。

Conclusion: 研究强调了直接轨道杂化的关键作用，指出了传统单点Kondo杂质模型的局限性，并表明在团簇Mott绝缘体中可能实现对纠缠相互作用的分别调控。

Abstract: Magnetic impurities in strongly correlated electronic systems serve as sensitive probes to a wide range of many-body quantum phenomena. Broken symmetries in such a system can lead to inequivalent lattice sites, and magnetic impurities may interact selectively with particular orbitals or sublattices. However, the microscopic mechanisms behind such site-specific interactions have been poorly understood. Here, we explore the behavior of individual Fe adatoms on a cluster-Mott charge-density-wave (CDW) system of 1T-TaS2 utilizing scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) and density functional theory (DFT). Our measurements uncover pronounced site-dependent electronic states of CDW clusters with Fe adatoms, indicating distinct local coupling to cluster-Mott states. DFT calculations identify three distinct types of interactions; hybridization with localized correlated electrons, distorting the CDW cluster, and charge transfer. In particular, the hybridization of Fe 3d and half-filled Ta 5dz2 orbitals suppresses the Mott insulating state for an adatom at the center of a CDW cluster. While the results underscore a crucial role of the direct orbital hybridization and the limitation of the prevailing single-site Kondo impurity model, they suggest the possibility of controlling entangled interactions separately in a cluster Mott insulator.

</details>


### [131] [Intriguing Magnetocaloric Effect in 6H-perovskite Ba3RRu2O9 (R=Ho, Gd, Tb, Nd) with Strong 4d-4f Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24758)
*Mohit Kumar,Sayan Ghosh,Gourab Roy,Ekta Kushwaha,Vincent Caignaert,Wilfrid Prellier,Subham Majumdar,Vincent Hardy,Tathamay Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究展示了Ba3RRu2O9系列化合物（R=Ho、Gd、Tb、Nd）的磁热效应，发现重稀土成员在低温磁相变附近表现出从常规到非常规磁热效应的转变，而轻稀土成员Nd在铁磁有序附近表现出正磁热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究4d-4f关联系统Ba3RRu2O9的磁热效应，探索不同稀土元素对磁有序行为和磁热性能的影响，特别是重稀土和轻稀土成员之间的差异。

Method: 通过合成Ba3RRu2O9系列化合物（R=Ho、Gd、Tb、Nd），研究其磁有序温度、相变行为，并测量磁热效应特性，分析温度依赖的复杂自旋重取向和各向异性。

Result: Ba3HoRu2O9在50K反铁磁有序，10K附近有另一相变；Ba3GdRu2O9和Ba3TbRu2O9分别在14.5K和10.5K有序。重稀土成员在低温磁相变附近表现出从常规到非常规磁热效应的转变，而Ba3NdRu2O9在24K以下铁磁有序，在铁磁有序附近表现出正磁热效应。

Conclusion: Ba3RRu2O9系列化合物表现出丰富的磁热效应行为，重稀土成员的非常规磁热效应转变和轻稀土成员的正磁热效应归因于温度依赖的复杂自旋重取向和各向异性，为磁制冷材料设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Here we demonstrate the magnetocaloric effect (MCE) of a 4d-4f correlated system, namely Ba3RRu2O9 (R= Ho, Gd, Tb, Nd). The compound Ba3HoRu2O9 antiferromagnetically orders at 50 K where both the Ho and Ru-moments order, followed by another phase transition ~ 10 K. Whereas, the compound Ba3GdRu2O9 and Ba3TbRu2O9 orders at 14.5 and 10.5 K respectively, where the ordering of both R and Ru moments are speculated. Our results reveal robust MCE around low-T magnetic phase transition for all the heavy rare-earth members (Ho, Gd, Tb) in this family. The heavy rare-earth members exhibit an intriguing MCE behavior switching from conventional to non-conventional MCE. Interestingly, the light R-member, Ba3NdRu2O9, orders ferromagnetically below 24 K where Nd-moments order, followed by Ru-ordering below 18 K, exhibits a positive MCE below and above FM-ordering. The compelling MCE are attributed to temperature dependent complex spin-reorientations for different R-members and anisotropy.

</details>


### [132] [Quasiparticle Dynamics in the 4d-4f Ising-like Double Perovskite Ba2DyRuO6 Probed by Neutron Scattering and Machine-Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24778)
*Gourab Roy,Ekta Kushwaha,Mohit Kumar,Sayan Ghosh,Fabio Orlandi,Duc Le,Matthew B. Stone,Jhuma Sannigrahi,Devashibhai T. Adroja,Tathamay Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ba₂DyRuO₆双钙钛矿中4d-4f相互作用导致47K下长程反铁磁有序，Dy和Ru同时磁有序，具有Ising特征。通过中子散射和理论建模揭示了复杂的交换相互作用、磁各向异性以及晶体场激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双钙钛矿Ba₂DyRuO₆中4d-4f相互作用的磁基态和准粒子激发，解释其独特的单磁相变行为（与同族其他稀土成员通常显示Ru离子先有序、R离子后有序的双相变不同）。

Method: 采用飞行时间中子衍射(TOF)、非弹性中子散射(INS)、拉曼光谱、理论建模（SpinW模拟）、点电荷计算和机器学习方法分析声子谱。

Result: 在TN≈47K出现长程反铁磁有序，Dy和Ru同时有序，形成具有Ising特征的共线反铁磁体（μRu=1.6μB，μDy=5.1μB）。INS显示10meV以下的磁振子激发，SpinW建模揭示复杂交换相互作用和磁各向异性。INS和拉曼光谱在顺磁区观察到Dy³⁺的晶体场激发（46.5和71.8meV），与Oh对称性一致。

Conclusion: Ba₂DyRuO₆的磁基态由4d-4f Ru⁵⁺-Dy³⁺交换相互作用驱动，Dy和Ru同时有序，磁各向异性导致Ising基态。研究阐明了声子和磁振子激发及其在基态磁性中的作用。

Abstract: Double perovskites containing 4d--4f interactions provide a platform to study complex magnetic phenomena in correlated systems. Here, we investigate the magnetic ground state and quasiparticle excitations of the fascinating double perovskite system, Ba$_2$DyRuO$_6$, through Time of flight (TOF) neutron diffraction (TOF), inelastic neutron scattering (INS), and theoretical modelling. The compound Ba$_2$DyRuO$_6$ is reported to exhibit a single magnetic transition, in sharp contrast to most of the other rare-earth (R) members in this family, A$_2$RRuO$_6$ (A = Ca/Sr/Ba), which typically show magnetic ordering of the Ru ions, followed by R-ion ordering. Our neutron diffraction results confirm that long-range antiferromagnetic order emerges at $T_\mathrm{N} \approx 47$~K, primarily driven by 4d--4f Ru$^{5+}$--Dy$^{3+}$ exchange interactions, where both Dy and Ru moments start to order simultaneously. The ordered ground state is a collinear antiferromagnet with Ising character, carrying ordered moments of $μ_{\mathrm{Ru}} = 1.6(1)~μ_\mathrm{B}$ and $μ_{\mathrm{Dy}} = 5.1(1)~μ_\mathrm{B}$ at 1.5~K. Low-temperature INS reveals well-defined magnon excitations below 10~meV. SpinW modelling of the INS spectra evidences complex exchange interactions and the presence of magnetic anisotropy, which governs the Ising ground state and accounts for the observed magnon spectrum. Combined INS and Raman spectroscopy reveal crystal-electric-field (CEF) excitations of Dy$^{3+}$ at 46.5 and 71.8~meV in the paramagnetic region. The observed CEF levels are reproduced by point-charge calculations consistent with the $O_h$ symmetry of Dy$^{3+}$. A complementary machine-learning approach is used to analyse the phonon spectrum and compare with INS data. Together, these results clarify the origin of phonon and magnon excitations and their role in the ground-state magnetism of Ba$_2$DyRuO$_6$.

</details>


### [133] [Parity order as a fundamental driver of bosonic topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25011)
*Ashirbad Padhan,Harsh Nigam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文发现奇偶序与键二聚化的耦合是驱动玻色子拓扑的新机制，在相互作用玻色系统中产生了两种新的拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管相互作用玻色系统中的对称保护拓扑相已被广泛研究，但大多数实现依赖于精细调节的相互作用或扩大的对称性。需要寻找新的机制来驱动玻色子拓扑。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重整化群模拟，研究奇偶序与键二聚化耦合的模型，识别不同于纯二聚化模型的新拓扑相。

Result: 发现了两种新的拓扑相：在正奇偶耦合下稳定的半填充SPT相，以及在负耦合下稳定的单位填充拓扑相，后者可以在不破坏任何对称性的情况下绝热连接到平凡相。

Conclusion: 奇偶序是关联驱动玻色子拓扑的新组织原理，为理解玻色系统中的拓扑相提供了新机制。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in interacting bosonic systems have been extensively studied, yet most realizations rely on fine-tuned interactions or enlarged symmetries. Here we show that a qualitatively different mechanism--parity order coupled to bond dimerization--acts as a fundamental driver of bosonic topology. Using density matrix renormalization group simulations, we identify two distinct topological phases absent in the purely dimerized model: an SPT phase at half filling stabilized by positive parity coupling, and a topological phase at unit filling stabilized by negative coupling that can be adiabatically connected to a trivial phase without breaking any symmetry. Our results establish parity order as a new organizing principle for correlation-driven bosonic topology.

</details>


### [134] [Perturbative Kondo destruction and global phase diagram of heavy fermion metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25036)
*Yiming Wang,Shouvik Sur,Chia-Chuan Liu,Qimiao Si*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种统一的场论方法，从磁性有序侧分析重费米子系统中Kondo效应与局域磁矩磁性的竞争，首次在重整化群框架内揭示了Kondo破坏量子临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属是量子凝聚态物理中的基础问题，重费米子系统为推进一般理解提供了典型平台。Kondo破坏量子临界点概念被广泛用于描述Kondo效应与局域磁矩磁性的竞争，但需要从磁性有序侧建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 开发了一种统一的场论方法，从磁性有序侧分析Kondo效应与局域磁矩磁性的竞争。该方法基于重整化群框架，首次在理论框架内揭示了Kondo破坏量子临界点。

Result: 分析首次在重整化群框架内揭示了一个量子临界点，在该临界点上Kondo效应从被破坏转变为占主导地位。这一发现不仅阐明了Kondo破坏量子临界性，还揭示了重费米子金属的伴随全局相图。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解重费米子系统中奇异金属行为提供了新的理论框架，首次从磁性有序侧在重整化群框架内揭示了Kondo破坏量子临界点，对重费米子金属的全局相图有重要启示。

Abstract: Strange metals represent a foundational problem in quantum condensed matter physics, and heavy fermion systems provide a canonical setting to advance a general understanding. The concept of a Kondo destruction quantum critical point is widely invoked to describe the competition of the Kondo effect and the local-moment magnetism. Here, we develop a unified field-theoretic approach, analyzing this competition from a rare approach that is anchored by the magnetically ordered side. Our analysis reveals, for the first time within a renormalization group framework, a quantum critical point across which the Kondo effect goes from being destroyed to dominating. Our findings elucidate not only the Kondo destruction quantum criticality but also an accompanying global phase diagram of heavy fermion metals.

</details>


### [135] [Emergence of 3D Superconformal Ising Criticality on the Fuzzy Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25054)
*Yin Tang,Cristian Voinea,Liangdong Hu,Zlatko Papić,W. Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过模糊球正则化方法，实现了三维N=1超共形Ising临界点的微观非微扰构造，揭示了超对称多重态结构和重整化群流


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称共形场论（SCFTs）为理解强耦合临界现象提供了独特视角，但三维N=1超共形Ising临界点的微观非微扰实现一直缺乏。本文旨在通过Yukawa型耦合构建这一临界点，并直接提取低能算符的标度维数。

Method: 采用模糊球正则化方法，构建三维Ising CFT与规范Majorana费米子之间的Yukawa型耦合模型。利用状态-算符对应关系直接提取低能算符的标度维数，通过调节Yukawa耦合追踪从解耦Ising-Majorana固定点到相互作用超共形固定点的算符谱演化。

Result: 在临界点处观测到共形多重态结构，并通过费米子与玻色子算符之间的特征关系证实了涌现的时空超对称性。成功追踪了算符谱从解耦固定点到超共形固定点的演化，揭示了算符层面的重整化群流。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一条受控的、非微扰的微观路径来实现三维SCFTs，为理解强耦合超对称临界现象提供了新的数值方法基础。

Abstract: Supersymmetric conformal field theories (SCFTs) form a unique subset of quantum field theories which provide powerful insights into strongly coupled critical phenomena. Here, we present a microscopic and non-perturbative realization of the three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal Ising critical point, based on a Yukawa-type coupling between a 3D Ising CFT and a gauged Majorana fermion. Using the recently developed fuzzy sphere regularization, we directly extract the scaling dimensions of low-lying operators via the state-operator correspondence. At the critical point, we demonstrate conformal multiplet structure together with the hallmark of emergent spacetime supersymmetry through characteristic relations between fermionic and bosonic operators. Moreover, by tuning the Yukawa coupling, we explicitly track the evolution of operator spectra from the decoupled Ising-Majorana fixed point to the interacting superconformal fixed point, revealing renormalization-group flow at the operator level. Our results establish a controlled, non-perturbative microscopic route to 3D SCFTs.

</details>


### [136] [Classification of Interacting Topological Crystalline Superconductors in Three Dimensions and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25069)
*Shang-Qiang Ning,Xing-Yu Ren,Qing-Rui Wang,Yang Qi,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个系统分类三维相互作用拓扑超导体（TSC）的框架，特别是受晶体对称性保护的TSC，并应用于230个空间群的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自由费米子拓扑超导体的分类已经建立，但系统理解三维相互作用TSC的分类仍然困难，特别是那些受晶体对称性（如230个空间群）保护的TSC。需要建立通用框架来系统分类受晶体对称性和离散内禀对称性共同保护的三维相互作用TSC。

Method: 首先建立具有纯离散内禀对称性的费米子对称保护拓扑相（FSPT）的完整分类，通过晶体等价原理确定晶体对称情况。使用畴壁装饰方法获得通用FSPT的分类数据和公式，适合系统计算。四层装饰数据$(n_1, n_2, n_3, ν_4)$表征具有对称性$G_b×_{ω_2}Z_2^f$的三维FSPT，对应$p+ip$、Kitaev链、复费米子和玻色子SPT层。特别关注$p+ip$层和Kitaev链装饰层。

Result: 获得了所有三层装饰$(n_2, n_3, ν_4)$的显式公式，适合自动计算。展示了反幺正对称性的$p+ip$层分类映射到某些镜面FSPT分类，而无限阶对称性（如平移）由$H^1(G_b, Z_T)$的自由部分确定，对应弱TSC。Kitaev链装饰对于反幺正对称性与幺正对称性有本质不同。

Conclusion: 建立了一个通用框架来系统分类三维相互作用拓扑超导体，特别是受晶体对称性保护的TSC。作为应用，分类了相互作用电子系统中230个空间群的拓扑晶体超导体，为理解相互作用TSC提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Although classification for free-fermion topological superconductors (TSC) is established, systematically understanding the classification of 3D interacting TSCs remains difficult, especially those protected by crystalline symmetries like the 230 space groups. We build up a general framework for systematically classifying 3D interacting TSCs protected by crystalline symmetries together with discrete internal symmetries. We first establish a complete classification for fermionic symmetry protected topological phases (FSPT) with purely discrete internal symmetries, which determines the crystalline case via the crystalline equivalence principle. Using domain wall decoration, we obtain classification data and formulas for generic FSPTs, what are suitable for systematic computation. The four layers of decoration data $(n_1, n_2, n_3, ν_4)$ characterize a 3D FSPT with symmetry $G_b\times_{ω_2}Z_2^f$, corresponding to $p+ip$, Kitaev chain, complex fermion, and bosonic SPT layers. Inspired by previous works, a crucial aspect is the $p+ip$ layer, where classification involves two possibilities: anti-unitary and infinite-order symmetries (e.g., translation). We show the former maps to some mirror FSPT classification with the mirror plane decorated by a $p+ip$ superconductor, while the latter is determined by the free part of $H^1(G_b, Z_T)$, corresponding to weak TSCs. Another key point is the Kitaev chain decoration for the anti-unitary symmetries, which differs essentially from unitary ones. We explicitly obtain formulas for all three layers of decoration $(n_2, n_3, ν_4)$, which are amenable to automatic computation. As an application, we classify the 230 space-group topological crystalline superconductors in interacting electronic systems.

</details>

### [137] [Pair Space in Classical Mechanics III. Some Four-Body Central Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23730)
*Alon Drory*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究四体问题中的中心构型，利用对空间形式主义推导完全表征此类构型的矢量方程组，重点分析至少两对体间距离相等的子类解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四体问题中的中心构型，即所有天体受力都指向空间中固定单点的构型，探索具有特定几何对称性的特殊构型

Method: 采用新提出的对空间形式主义，推导出完全表征中心构型的矢量方程组，重点分析至少两对体间距离相等的子类解

Result: 发现仅有的非共线构型包括四面体（唯一的非平面构型）、风筝形和等腰梯形，具体形状（内角）由天体质量比决定，给出了所有这些关系的数学表达式

Conclusion: 四体问题中至少两对体间距离相等的中心构型只有三种非共线类型，其几何形状完全由质量比决定，为理解四体系统的对称构型提供了完整数学描述

Abstract: We study central configurations in the four body problem, i.e., configurations in which the forces on all the bodies point to a fixed, single point in space. The newly formulated pair-space formalism yields a set of vectorial equations that fully characterize such configurations. We investigate a sub-class of solutions in which at least two pairs of inter-body distances are equal. The only such non-collinear configurations are the tetrahedron (the unique non-planar configuration), kites and the isosceles trapezium. The specific shapes (internal angles) are determined by the ratio of the masses of the bodies. Mathematical expression are given for all these relations.

</details>


### [138] [Adjoint L-Infinity Actions and Conserved Charges in GR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23970)
*Changsun Choi,Ryan E. Grady*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过L∞代数方法计算爱因斯坦-嘉当-帕拉蒂尼引力中的守恒流和守恒荷，并应用于计算史瓦西黑洞熵


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究爱因斯坦-嘉当-帕拉蒂尼引力理论中与无穷小等距变换（Killing场）相关的守恒流和守恒荷，为引力理论中的守恒律提供新的数学框架

Method: 采用L∞代数形式体系和Ćirić、Giotopoulos、Radovanović、Szabo以及Costello和Gwilliam的工作，提出计算守恒量的新方法，并证明关于L∞代数伴随作用存在性和实用性的代数结果

Result: 成功计算了爱因斯坦-嘉当-帕拉蒂尼引力中的守恒流和守恒荷，并具体应用于计算史瓦西黑洞的熵，验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: L∞代数方法为引力理论中的守恒量计算提供了新的数学框架，该方法不仅适用于爱因斯坦-嘉当-帕拉蒂尼引力，还可推广到其他引力理论，具有重要的理论价值

Abstract: In this work we compute the conserved currents and charges associated to the action of an infinitesimal isometry (Killing field) in Einstein--Cartan--Palatini gravity. We offer a new approach to these quantities through the formalism of $L_\infty$-algebras and the work of Ćirić, Giotopoulos, Radovanović, and Szabo, and Costello and Gwilliam. We demonstrate our approach by computing the entropy of the Schwarzchild black hole. Along the way, we prove a purely algebraic result about the existence and utility of a higher (a full $\infty$) version of the adjoint action of an $L_\infty$-algebra.

</details>


### [139] [On spectral equations for an evolution operator of a $q$-oscillator lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24043)
*Sergey Sergeev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出描述二维q-振荡器Kagomé晶格相对论演化算符特征值和特征态的代数方程组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维可积量子力学系统中的相对论演化算符，关注其幺正性区域

Method: 基于四面体方程的q-振荡器解构造演化算符，采用二维晶格版本的坐标Bethe-Ansatz方法

Result: 提出了描述特征值和特征态的代数方程组，建立了与三维可积量子力学系统的联系

Conclusion: 成功构建了二维q-振荡器Kagomé晶格相对论演化算符的代数描述，为三维可积系统研究提供了新方法

Abstract: We propose a set of algebraic equations describing eigenvalues and eigenstates of a relativistic evolution operator for a two-dimensional $q$-oscillator Kagomé lattice. Evolution operator is constructed with the help of $q$-oscillator solution of the Tetrahedron Equation. We focus on the unitary regime of the evolution operator, so our results are related to 3d integrable systems of the quantum mechanics. Our conjecture is based on a two-dimensional lattice version of the coordinate Bethe-Ansatz.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum two-dimensional superintegrable systems in flat space: exact-solvability, hidden algebra, polynomial algebra of integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24045)
*Alexander V Turbiner,Juan Carlos Lopez Vieyra,Pavel Winternitz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文综述了六个二维量子超可积系统，证明它们都是精确可解的，具有代数形式的哈密顿量和积分，包含多项式本征函数和隐藏代数结构，并形成无限维多项式代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证蒙特利尔猜想（2001），即某些二维量子超可积系统是精确可解的，并系统分析这些系统的代数结构和数学特性。

Method: 详细分析六个二维量子超可积系统：Smorodinsky-Winternitz势I-II（Holt势）、Fokas-Lagerstrom模型、3体Calogero和Wolfes模型（等价于G2有理或I6模型）、以及整数指标k的TTW系统。

Result: 所有六个系统都是精确可解的，具有代数形式的哈密顿量和积分（微分算子，多项式系数无常数项），多项式本征函数，隐藏代数结构g^(k)，以及有限阶多项式积分代数。每个模型都有无限多个有限维不变子空间，形成无限旗结构。

Conclusion: 这些二维量子超可积系统证实了蒙特利尔猜想，它们具有丰富的代数结构：代数形式的哈密顿量和积分、多项式本征函数、隐藏代数结构、多项式积分代数，以及无限旗不变子空间，这些子空间与隐藏代数的有限维表示空间重合。

Abstract: In this short review paper the detailed analysis of six two-dimensional quantum {\it superintegrable} systems in flat space is presented. It includes the Smorodinsky-Winternitz potentials I-II (the Holt potential), the Fokas-Lagerstrom model, the 3-body Calogero and Wolfes (equivalently, $G_2$ rational, or $I_6$) models, and the Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW) system with integer index $k$. It is shown that all of them are exactly-solvable, thus, confirming the Montreal conjecture (2001); they admit algebraic forms for the Hamiltonian and both integrals (all three can be written as differential operators with polynomial coefficients without a constant term), they have polynomial eigenfunctions with the invariants of the discrete symmetry group of invariance taken as variables, they have hidden (Lie) algebraic structure $g^{(k)}$ with various $k$, and they possess a (finite order) polynomial algebras of integrals. Each model is characterized by infinitely-many finite-dimensional invariant subspaces, which form the infinite flag. Each subspace coincides with the finite-dimensional representation space of the algebra $g^{(k)}$ for a certain $k$. In all presented cases the algebra of integrals is a 4-generated $(H, I_1, I_2, I_{12}\equiv[I_1, I_2])$ infinite-dimensional algebra of ordered monomials of degrees 2,3,4,5, which is a subalgebra of the universal enveloping algebra of the hidden algebra.

</details>


### [141] [Charge functions for all dimensional partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24343)
*Hao Feng,Tian-Shun Chen,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出了任意偶数维分划的电荷函数公式猜想，并在6D严格证明、8D数值验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知2D、3D、4D分划的电荷函数表达式，近期工作给出了任意奇数维的表达式，需要进一步研究偶数维情况

Method: 提出偶数维分划电荷函数的猜想公式，对6维情况给出严格证明，对8维情况进行数值验证

Result: 成功推导出偶数维分划电荷函数的猜想表达式，6维情况得到严格证明，8维情况通过数值验证支持猜想

Conclusion: 提出了任意偶数维分划电荷函数的完整猜想，为高维分划理论提供了新的数学工具

Abstract: The charge functions for n-dimensional partitions are known for n=2,3,4 in the literature. We give the expression for arbitrary odd dimension in a recent work, and now further conjecture a formula for all even dimensional cases. This conjecture is proved rigorously for 6D, and numerically verified for 8D.

</details>


### [142] [Quasicrystalline Gibbs states in 4-dimensional lattice-gas models with finite-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24436)
*Siamak Taati,Jacek Miȩkisz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 构建了一个具有有限范围相互作用的四维晶格气体模型，该模型在低温下具有非周期性的"准晶"吉布斯态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有非周期性基态构型的统计力学系统，探索准晶态在热力学极限下的稳定性

Method: 基于概率元胞自动机与其时空轨迹上吉布斯测度的对应关系，以及噪声鲁棒计算的经典结果。利用Ammann非周期铺砖构造元胞自动机，通过两个额外维度的重复和纠错机制确保轨迹在噪声下的稳定性

Result: 成功构造了四维晶格气体模型，在低温下存在非周期性"准晶"吉布斯态，这些态是Ammann非周期铺砖对应基态构型的小扰动

Conclusion: 证明了有限范围相互作用系统可以支持稳定的非周期性吉布斯态，为准晶态在统计力学框架下的研究提供了理论模型

Abstract: We construct a four-dimensional lattice-gas model with finite-range interactions that has non-periodic, ``quasicrystalline'' Gibbs states at low temperatures. Such Gibbs states are probability measures which are small perturbations of non-periodic ground-state configurations corresponding to tilings of the plane with Ammann's aperiodic tiles. Our construction is based on the correspondence between probabilistic cellular automata and Gibbs measures on their space-time trajectories, and a classical result on noise-resilient computing with cellular automata. The cellular automaton is constructed on the basis of Ammann's tiles, which are deterministic in one direction, and has non-periodic space-time trajectories corresponding to each valid tiling. Repetitions along two extra dimensions, together with an error-correction mechanism, ensure stability of the trajectories subjected to noise.

</details>


### [143] [From Yang-Mills to Yang-Baxter: In Memory of Rodney Baxter and Chen--Ning Yang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24494)
*Bai-Ling Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文纪念杨振宁和罗德尼·巴克斯特，探讨规范场论与可积系统之间的深刻联系，展示局部一致性原理如何生成全局数学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纪念2025年逝世的二十世纪数学物理学两位巨匠——罗德尼·巴克斯特和陈宁·杨（杨振宁），并探讨他们工作揭示的规范场论与可积系统之间的深刻联系。杨振宁通过引入非阿贝尔规范场论和杨-巴克斯特方程的一致性条件重塑了现代物理学，而巴克斯特将这些条件转化为统计力学和量子可积系统中精确可解性的系统理论。

Method: 采用回顾性综述方法，从两个平行但互补的视角展开：一方面回顾杨-米尔斯规范场论的表述、质量障碍及其通过对称性破缺的解决方案，以及由此产生的几何框架（包括瞬子、唐纳森-弗洛尔理论、磁单极子和希钦系统）；另一方面追溯杨-巴克斯特方程从因子化散射到可解晶格模型、量子群和陈-西蒙斯理论的发展历程。

Result: 论文展示了规范场论和可积性并非独立叙事，而是共享一致性原理的互补表现形式。通过系统梳理两位科学家的贡献，揭示了局部一致性原理如何生成全局数学结构，以及从规范对称性走向数学统一性的持续旅程。

Conclusion: 杨振宁和巴克斯特的工作共同揭示了规范场论与可积系统之间的深刻统一性，表明它们都是基于局部一致性原理的数学结构的不同表现。这种联系不仅具有理论意义，而且为理解物理系统的深层数学结构提供了统一框架，代表了从规范对称性到数学统一性的持续科学探索。

Abstract: The year 2025 marked the passing of two towering figures of twentieth-century mathematical physics, Rodney Baxter and Chen-Ning Yang. Yang reshaped modern physics through the introduction of non-abelian gauge theory and, independently, through the consistency conditions underlying what is now called the Yang-Baxter equation. Baxter transformed those conditions into a systematic theory of exact solvability in statistical mechanics and quantum integrable systems. This article is written in memory of Baxter and Yang, whose work revealed how local consistency principles generate global mathematical structure. We review the Yang-Mills formulation of gauge theory, its mass obstruction and resolution via symmetry breaking, and the geometric framework it engendered, including instantons, Donaldson-Floer theory, magnetic monopoles, and Hitchin systems. In parallel, we trace the emergence of the Yang-Baxter equation from factorised scattering to solvable lattice models, quantum groups, and Chern-Simons theory. Rather than separate narratives, gauge theory and integrability are presented as complementary manifestations of a shared coherence principle, an ongoing journey from gauge symmetry toward mathematical unity.

</details>


### [144] [Collective behaviors of an electron gas in the mean-field regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24666)
*Dong Hao Ou Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究三维环面上电子气的动量分布，通过随机相位近似计算试验态的傅里叶模式占据数，得到高密度极限和金属密度下的平均场动量分布公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电子气在三维环面上的动量分布，旨在通过随机相位近似方法计算试验态的傅里叶模式占据数，扩展相关理论结果。

Method: 使用随机相位近似方法获得试验态，计算电子气在三维环面上的动量分布（傅里叶模式占据数），分析高密度极限和金属密度情况。

Result: 得到了高密度极限下（Daniel和Voskov, 1960）和金属密度下（Lam, 1971）的平均场动量分布公式，适用于一般奇异势而不仅是库仑势。

Conclusion: 研究结果与Benedikter、Lill和Naidu的近期工作相关，且分析方法适用于更广泛的奇异势类别，扩展了电子气动量分布的理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the momentum distribution of an electron gas in a $3$-dimensional torus. The goal is to compute the occupation number of Fourier modes for some trial state obtained through random phase approximation. We obtain the mean-field analogue of momentum distribution formulas for electron gas in [Daniel and Voskov, Phys. Rev. 120, (1960)] in high density limit and [Lam, Phys. Rev. \textbf{3}, (1971)] at metallic density. Our findings are related to recent results obtained independently by Benedikter, Lill and Naidu, and our analysis applies to a general class of singular potentials rather than just the Coulomb case.

</details>


### [145] [Products of random Hermitian matrices and brickwork Hurwitz numbers. Products of normal matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24720)
*Ch. Li,A. Yu. Orlov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了n个随机厄米矩阵的乘积，将其与计数特定类型分支覆盖的Hurwitz数联系起来，并考虑了正态随机矩阵的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机矩阵乘积模型，探索其与代数几何中Hurwitz数的深刻联系，将随机矩阵理论与分支覆盖计数问题相结合。

Method: 考虑n个随机厄米矩阵的乘积，推广单矩阵模型；分析该模型与Hurwitz数的关系，这些Hurwitz数计数黎曼球面上具有特定分支类型的2k重分支覆盖。

Result: 建立了随机矩阵乘积模型与Hurwitz数的对应关系，具体对应计数黎曼球面上在0和∞处具有任意分支类型、在其他位置具有(2^k)分区相关分支（"砖砌"结构）的2k重分支覆盖。

Conclusion: 随机厄米矩阵乘积模型与特定Hurwitz数存在深刻联系，为随机矩阵理论与代数几何分支覆盖理论的交叉研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We consider products of $n$ random Hermitian matrices which generalize the one-matrix model and show its relation to Hurwitz numbers which count ramified coverings of certain type. Namely, these Hurwitz numbers count $2k$-fold ramified coverings of the Riemann sphere with arbitrary ramification type over $0$ and $\infty$ and ramifications related to the partition $(2^k)$ (``brickworks'' - involution without fixed points) elsewhere. Products of normal random matrices are also considered.

</details>


### [146] [Geometric approaches to Lie bialgebras, their classification, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24795)
*Daniel Wysocki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该博士论文分为两部分：第一部分研究上边缘李双代数的分类问题，提出代数与几何方法；第二部分探讨r-矩阵在叶状李-哈密顿系统和李系统变形中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究上边缘李双代数的分类问题，特别是通过李代数自同构对r-矩阵的等价类进行分类。同时探索r-矩阵在叶状李-哈密顿系统和李系统变形中的应用价值。

Method: 第一部分采用代数方法（格拉斯曼代数、分次代数、代数不变量技术）和几何方法（新引入的达布族概念）。第二部分应用r-矩阵研究叶状李-哈密顿系统，并基于李-哈密顿系统变形的一般程序，将其扩展到雅可比-李系统。

Result: 成功研究了三维上边缘李双代数r-矩阵的等价类，部分分析了高维情况如𝔰𝔬(2,2)和𝔰𝔬(3,2)。达布族方法有效研究了四维不可分解上边缘李双代数r-矩阵的等价类，并以𝔤𝔩₂为例展示了其在可分解情况的应用。提出了雅可比-李系统变形的扩展，并通过施瓦茨方程的详细计算得到支持。

Conclusion: 论文发展了上边缘李双代数分类的新代数与几何方法，特别是达布族概念为研究r-矩阵等价类提供了强大工具。同时展示了r-矩阵在叶状李-哈密顿系统和李系统变形中的潜在应用价值，为相关领域研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: This PhD Thesis consists of two parts. The first part focuses on novel algebraic and geometric approaches to the classification problem of coboundary Lie bialgebras up to Lie algebra automorphisms. More specifically, Grassmann, graded algebra and algebraic invariant techniques are discussed. Using these algebraic methods, equivalence classes of r-matrices for three-dimensional coboundary Lie bialgebras are studied. Moreover, particular higher-dimensional cases, e.g. $\mathfrak{so}(2,2)$ and $\mathfrak{so}(3,2)$, are partially analysed. From the geometric perspective, the main role is played by the newly introduced notion: the Darboux family. This powerful tool allows an efficient and thorough study of equivalence classes of r-matrices for four-dimensional indecomposable coboundary Lie bialgebras. In order to showcase its ability to tackle decomposable examples, $\mathfrak{gl}_2$ is additionally studied. The second part of the Thesis sketches interesting directions for applications of r-matrices. Firstly, it is illustrated how r-matrices might be useful to describe foliated Lie-Hamilton systems. Secondly, the role of r-matrices in deformations of certain cases of Lie systems is discussed. In particular, based on the general procedure for deformations of Lie-Hamilton systems, its extension to Jacobi-Lie systems is suggested and supported by the detailed computation of the deformed Schwarz equation.

</details>


### [147] [Numerical study of solitary waves in Dirac--Klein--Gordon system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24954)
*Andrew Comech,Julien Ricaud,Marco Roque*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值方法构造Dirac-Klein-Gordon模型中的孤立波，分析能量和电荷对频率ω的依赖关系，并讨论孤立波谱稳定性的可能含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirac-Klein-Gordon模型中孤立波的特性，特别是能量和电荷随频率ω的变化关系，以及这些孤立波的谱稳定性问题。

Method: 采用数值方法构造孤立波：1）使用迭代程序，从非线性Dirac方程的孤立波出发，计算相应的标量场，并调整耦合常数；2）对于无质量标量场情况，将迭代法与打靶法进行比较；3）利用维里恒等式控制模拟误差。

Result: 成功构造了一维和三维空间中的Dirac-Klein-Gordon孤立波，获得了能量和电荷随频率ω变化的依赖关系，并验证了迭代方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为Dirac-Klein-Gordon模型中孤立波的数值构造提供了有效方法，所得结果对理解这些孤立波的谱稳定性具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We use numerics to construct solitary waves in Dirac--Klein--Gordon (in one and three spatial dimensions) and study the dependence of energy and charge on $ω$. For the construction, we use the iterative procedure, starting from solitary waves of nonlinear Dirac equation, computing the corresponding scalar field, and adjusting the coupling constant. We also consider the case of massless scalar field, when the iteration procedure could be compared with the shooting method. We use the virial identities to control the error of simulations. We also discuss possible implications from the obtained results for the spectral stability of solitary waves.

</details>


### [148] [Bilinear tau forms of quantum Painlevé equations and $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$ blowup relations in SUSY gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25051)
*Giulio Bonelli,Anton Shchechkin,Alessandro Tanzini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了正则量子化Painlevé方程的双线性tau形式，建立了与N=2超对称规范理论配分函数blowup关系的联系，完善了精炼Painlevé/规范理论对应字典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子Painlevé方程与N=2超对称规范理论之间的精确对应关系，特别是通过双线性tau形式连接规范理论的blowup关系，完善精炼Painlevé/规范理论字典。

Method: 推导正则量子化Painlevé方程的双线性tau形式，建立与ℂ²/ℤ₂ blowup关系的联系，通过量子非自治Painlevé哈密顿量的适当方程来固定对应字典，并分析量子Painlevé tau函数的对称结构。

Result: 成功推导了量子Painlevé方程的双线性tau形式，完全固定了精炼Painlevé/规范理论对应字典，描述了量子Painlevé tau函数的对称结构，并作为副产品获得了规范理论非平凡holonomy扇区的blowup关系。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了量子Painlevé方程与N=2超对称规范理论之间的精确对应，通过双线性tau形式和对称性分析完善了精炼Painlevé/规范理论字典，为两个领域提供了新的联系和理解。

Abstract: We derive bilinear tau forms of the canonically quantized Painlevé equations, thereby relating them to those previously obtained from the $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$ blowup relations for the $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric gauge theory partition functions on a general $Ω$-background. We fully fix the refined Painlevé/gauge theory dictionary by formulating the proper equations for the quantum nonautonomous Painlevé Hamiltonians. We also describe the symmetry structure of the quantum Painlevé tau functions and, as a byproduct of this analysis, obtain the $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$ blowup relations in the nontrivial holonomy sector of the gauge theory.

</details>
